# Agents · R · part 22

965 entries, Resume Tailor to review. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **Resume Tailor**: Use when the user wants a resume tailored to a specific job description or job link. Trigger phrases: 'tailor my resume', 'tailor resume for this JD', 'apply to this role', 'generate a resume for this job'. Ranks bullets from ResumeAgent/resume_base.md against the JD, asks about gaps, renders a PDF into ResumeAgent/roles/, and reviews the accepted resume for broken links and screen-through odds. ([HustleAura/CareerFleet](https://github.com/HustleAura/CareerFleet/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/Resume%20Tailor.agent.md))
- **Resume Verifier**: Verifies resume against playbook requirements. Runs ALL verification checks including character counts (via Bash wc -c for each bullet), structure validation, quality checks, and returns structured PASS/FAIL report. ([shashikirandevadiga/apply-pilot](https://github.com/shashikirandevadiga/apply-pilot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/resume-verifier.md) · ★5)
- **resume-agent**: Reads the version state file after a stop and says exactly where to continue. Use it when a run was interrupted. ([emadbarandan/Arsenale-Ai-Agent-Company](https://github.com/emadbarandan/Arsenale-Ai-Agent-Company/blob/HEAD/packs/autonomous-dev/agents/resume-agent.md) · ★1)
- **RESUME-AGENT**: Deterministic resume analysis inside the existing multi-agent boundary. Routed only from the `analyze_resume` intent. No new orchestrator, endpoint, provider, LLM layer, tool boundary, automation, persistence, memory, or background job is introduced. ([devflow-modules/devflow](https://github.com/devflow-modules/devflow/blob/HEAD/docs/career-suite/agents/RESUME-AGENT.md))
- **resume-analyser**: Analyzes a resume/CV for structure, ATS-compatibility, impact, keyword coverage, and gaps, AND produces a fully rewritten, ATS-optimized version saved as a new dated .docx in the same directory. Use when a user shares a resume file (.docx, .pdf, .txt) or pastes resume text and wants honest feedback, scoring, rewrites, or an improved resume file. Can also compare a resume against a target job description. ([rajamuthu85/job-hunt-pipeline-](https://github.com/rajamuthu85/job-hunt-pipeline-/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-analyser.md))
- **resume-analyst**: 이력서를 읽어 채용공고 탐색용 프로필(연차, 직무와 직무군 키, 핵심 역량, 검색어)을 만드는 에이전트. 개발 직군과 개발 밖 직군 이력서를 모두 다룬다. job-hunt 오케스트레이터가 Phase 1에서 호출한다. ([chan9yu/job-fit-harness](https://github.com/chan9yu/job-fit-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/resume-analyst.md) · ★1)
- **resume-analyzer**: Analyzes an interrupted subtask (worktree + commits + partial report from prior /execute run) and writes .task/resume-E-XXX.X.md — a structured continuation plan with confidence and recommendation. Used by /execute pre-execute analysis when user picks Resume. ([Dmatryus/dm-cc-assistant](https://github.com/Dmatryus/dm-cc-assistant/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-analyzer.md))
- **resume-analyzer**: Analyze user's career history and extract comprehensive insights ([JayKim88/claude-ai-engineering](https://github.com/JayKim88/claude-ai-engineering/blob/HEAD/plugins/career-compass/agents/resume-analyzer.md))
- **resume-analyzer**: Use this agent when the setup skill needs a one-time deep analysis of the user's resume to build their job-search profile, or when the user updates their resume and asks to re-analyze it. ([raymaredia28/MZuck](https://github.com/raymaredia28/MZuck/blob/HEAD/job-finder/agents/resume-analyzer.md))
- **resume-analyzer**: Use this agent when a candidate resume needs structured pre-interview analysis. Typical triggers include being dispatched by the interview-prep skill with resume text, a user asking for a deep resume evaluation before deciding whether to interview, and identifying which resume claims to verify in a live interview. ([unfallenwill/skills](https://github.com/unfallenwill/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/interview-prep/agents/resume-analyzer.md))
- **resume-architect**: Resume Architect - Resume creation, ATS optimization, accomplishment framing, tailoring to specific job descriptions. The primary builder. Use AFTER skills inventory and keyword research are complete. ([10Legs/career-architect-harness](https://github.com/10Legs/career-architect-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/resume-architect.md) · ★4)
- **resume-assistant**: Activate when the user wants to create, polish, score, customize, optimize, or export a resume or CV. Triggers on requests like "resume", "CV", "polish my resume", "score my resume", "tailor for this job", "optimize for ATS", "export resume to PDF/Word". ([infometa/workbuddyskills](https://github.com/infometa/workbuddyskills/blob/HEAD/experts/resume-assistant/agents/resume-assistant.md) · ★455 · 4 copies)
- **resume-auditor**: moai-career 플러그인의 읽기 전용 회의적 검수자. career-coach 에이전트 또는 career-* 스킬이 작성한 이력서·자기소개서 초안, 포트폴리오 프로젝트 설명, 면접 준비 키트, 채용 시장 주장을 독립적으로 검증합니다. 증거 기반 PASS/FAIL 판정을 반환하며, 파일은 편집하지 않습니다. ([modu-ai/moai-cowork](https://github.com/modu-ai/moai-cowork/blob/HEAD/plugins/moai-career/agents/resume-auditor.md) · ★317)
- **resume-auditor**: Read-only Resume Team auditor that returns an exhaustive PASS or FAIL verdict without editing the draft. ([jananthan30/Resume-Builder](https://github.com/jananthan30/Resume-Builder/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/resume-auditor.md) · ★96 · 2 copies)
- **resume-auditor**: You are the CareerOps resume-auditor. You perform a semantic quality pass on a generated resume AFTER the 8 deterministic Python gates have passed. Your job is to catch what code cannot: weak bullets, AI texture, redundancy, poor framing, and tone inconsistency. ([KalharPandya/careerops](https://github.com/KalharPandya/careerops/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **resume-auditor**: 简历审计专家：运行确定性审计脚本并做人工复核，输出 blocker/warning/nit 分级体检报告。适用于 /lhg-resume:resume-audit 与投递前终检。 ([lhg-plugs/lhg-resume](https://github.com/lhg-plugs/lhg-resume/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-auditor.md))
- **resume-builder**: Builds a resume from scratch by scanning user-specified folders/files for work history, gathering missing info, and generating a best-practice resume as markdown and PDF. ([JCodesMore/jobs-for-ai-agents](https://github.com/JCodesMore/jobs-for-ai-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-builder.md) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **resume-builder**: Generates tailored resumes and cover letters from job descriptions (JD). Analyzes JD requirements, selects relevant experience, rewrites bullets for ATS optimization, and produces .docx documents. ([rmaloneycvg/ai](https://github.com/rmaloneycvg/ai/blob/HEAD/copilot/.github/agents/resume-builder.agent.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **resume-builder**: 简历构建专家：按 lhg-rs-bullet 方法论逐条改写 bullet，生成投递版与详细版，维护版本 CHANGELOG。适用于 /lhg-resume:resume 的改写与版本阶段。 ([lhg-plugs/lhg-resume](https://github.com/lhg-plugs/lhg-resume/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-builder.md))
- **resume-builder**: Builds or tailors a resume from the career bank for a target profile or posting, then renders it to PDF and DOCX. Use when the user asks to build, update or tailor a resume. ([Manishvermadagar/job-search-os](https://github.com/Manishvermadagar/job-search-os/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/resume-builder.md))
- **resume-builder**: Read career journal files over a date range, cluster related accomplishments across days, apply XYZ formula transformation, score for quality, and produce structured resume sections. Optionally tailor output to a job description. ([momocow/claude-plugin-career](https://github.com/momocow/claude-plugin-career/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-builder.md))
- **resume-career-optimizer**: 사용자가 소프트웨어 엔지니어링 이력서나 경력 문서를 검토, 최적화 또는 개선하고자 할 때 이 에이전트를 사용하세요. 업무 경험을 더 잘 표현하고 싶거나, 기술적 설명을 다듬고 싶을 때, 또는 채용 기회를 위해 지원 자료를 준비할 때 도움이 됩니다. 예시: &lt;example&gt; Context: 사용자가 이력서를 업로드하고 피드백을 요청함 user: "제 이력서입니다. 시니어 개발자 포지션에 맞게 개선하는 것을 도와주실 수 있나요?" assistant: "사용자의 이력서를 분석하고 종합적인 최적화 권장 사항을 제공하기 위해 resume-career-optimizer 에이전트를 실행하겠습니다." &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: 사용자가 이력서에 복잡한 프로젝트를 기술하는 데 어려움을 겪고 있음 user: "마이크로서비스 마이그레이션 프로젝트를 진행했는데 이력서에 어떻게 효과적으로 적어야 할지 모르겠어요." assistant: "resume-career-optimizer 에이전트를 실행해, 마이크로서비스 마이그레이션 프로젝트를 성과 중심의 매력적인 설명으로 작성할 수 있도록 도와드리겠습니다." &lt;/example&gt; ([junsik/claude-plugins](https://github.com/junsik/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/job-ready/agents/resume-career-optimizer.md))
- **resume-career-strategist**: Use this agent when you need strategic analysis of a resume against a specific company's needs and culture. Examples:\n\n&lt;example&gt;\nContext: User has researched a company and wants to understand how their resume aligns with a product management role at that company.\nuser: "I've gathered research on Stripe's product management culture and their current focus areas. Can you analyze my resume against this research?"\nassistant: "I'm going to use the Task tool to launch the resume-career-strategist agent to provide a comprehensive analysis of your resume strengths, weaknesses, and interview stra… ([4qan/claude-job-pipeline](https://github.com/4qan/claude-job-pipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/resume-career-strategist.md))
- **resume-checker**: Checks a tailored resume for truthfulness against the candidate's master resume and confirmed answers, and returns a table of unsupported claims. Use after the job-application-agent skill drafts a tailored resume and before the user sees it. Give it four inputs in the prompt (MASTER, ANSWERS, POSTING, DRAFT) and nothing else. ([coder38/job-application-agent](https://github.com/coder38/job-application-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-checker.md))
- **resume-cluster**: Cheap read-only bounded inference agent for /z-resume. Receives one resume-context supplied cluster or compact top-cluster comparison and returns a cited JSON judgment without inspecting arbitrary repo state. ([zeke13dev/z-harness](https://github.com/zeke13dev/z-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-cluster.md))
- **resume-coach**: Resume content and layout specialist — one-page discipline, quantified achievement bullets, job-description tailoring, and ATS-safe formatting. Use when writing or revising a resume/CV, tightening it to one page, rewriting bullet points, tailoring a resume to a specific job posting, or checking ATS compatibility. ([igorilic/latex-agentic](https://github.com/igorilic/latex-agentic/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-coach.md))
- **resume-consultant-neutral**: Neutral, professional resume and cover letter consultant. Invoke for objective, unflinching feedback on CV bullets, cover letter drafts, LinkedIn summaries, or application materials. Use when you want the truth without cheerleading or harsh criticism. ([tkobler/Peronnal-Website](https://github.com/tkobler/Peronnal-Website/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/resume-consultant-neutral.md))
- **resume-consultant-supportive**: Encouraging, constructive resume and cover letter consultant. Invoke when you want feedback that builds momentum — good for early drafts, brainstorming how to frame achievements, or when morale is low. Pairs well with the grumpy-recruiter as a sanity check. ([tkobler/Peronnal-Website](https://github.com/tkobler/Peronnal-Website/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/resume-consultant-supportive.md))
- **resume-content-reviewer**: After any edit to data/resume.ts, validate that all required profile fields are present, all referenced image paths exist under assets/images/, and all URLs are well-formed. ([technotenshi/resume](https://github.com/technotenshi/resume/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/resume-content-reviewer.md))
- **resume-coordinator**: Persona for mugiwara-orchestration (resume). Rebuilds state from .mugiwara/missions/&lt;mission&gt;/state.json | &lt;member&gt;.json, continues never restarts. ([ionivetech/mugiwara](https://github.com/ionivetech/mugiwara/blob/HEAD/content/agents/resume-coordinator.md))
- **resume-creator**: Creates professional A4 resumes — asks what content/formatting the user wants, styles it with a clean design, and saves the PDF. ([whoabhaysharma/applier](https://github.com/whoabhaysharma/applier/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/resume-creator.md) · ★4)
- **resume-critic**: Adversarially checks a tailored CV for anything not supported by her master profile. Use during /apply before rendering the PDF. ([ahhshaikh/jobhunt-cos](https://github.com/ahhshaikh/jobhunt-cos/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/resume-critic.md))
- **resume-critic**: Reads a compiled resume PDF as a skeptical hiring manager and reports what is weak. Use after a resume draft compiles and passes fact-checking. ([imlamont/job-app](https://github.com/imlamont/job-app/blob/HEAD/job-app-opencode/.opencode/agents/resume-critic.md) · 2 copies)
- **resume-critic**: Independent content review of an already-rendered CV. Use after resume-writer returns and after the caller has independently re-run the deterministic gates, before any approval is offered. resume-critic reads the rendered markdown, the composition, the provenance sidecar and the cited source lines, then reviews the way a senior recruiter and a fact-checker would: duplicate and near-duplicate bullets, contradictions, inconsistent numbers, dates and titles across summary, highlights, skills and bullets, claims the cited source lines do not support, register slips, clumsy repeated prefixes, and… ([SharadKumar/job-hunt](https://github.com/SharadKumar/job-hunt/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-critic.md))
- **resume-customizer**: Tailors a base resume to match a specific company and job posting, using the company research brief. Outputs a customized resume as markdown and PDF. ([JCodesMore/jobs-for-ai-agents](https://github.com/JCodesMore/jobs-for-ai-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-customizer.md) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **resume-debats-assemblee**: Écrit le résumé des débats d'un texte de loi à l'Assemblée nationale, pour ce projet — au plus quatre arguments par groupe politique, tirés des seules prises de parole de ce groupe. Utiliser après `.claude/scripts/faits_pour_resumes.py`, pour produire le lot JSON que `.claude/scripts/assembler_resumes.py` contrôlera. Il n'écrit jamais un camp de vote et ne relie jamais une phrase d'intention au vote émis. ([YESNO584/AN-API](https://github.com/YESNO584/AN-API/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/resume-debats-assemblee.md))
- **resume-design-advisor**: Recommend a resume layout direction that supports the target reader without sacrificing ATS safety. ([JonathanKHobson/working-tools-plugins](https://github.com/JonathanKHobson/working-tools-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/career-studio/skills/resume/agents/resume-design-advisor.md))
- **resume-draft**: Selects which true bank material to surface for the JD and writes the tailored resume CONTENT in one pass. Reads the raw JD + evidence map + schema, plans the selection from the map, confirms the chosen material against the bank, then writes &lt;ws&gt;/.run/resume.json (the draft, against spec/resume_schema.json) and appends evidence gaps / adjacency to &lt;ws&gt;/gaps.md. Owns selection, prioritization, bullet craft, tone, and length. ([ledq/resumery](https://github.com/ledq/resumery/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/resume-draft.md) · ★2)
- **resume-editor**: Resume Team editor that returns one complete, narrowly corrected draft for explicit audit findings only. ([jananthan30/Resume-Builder](https://github.com/jananthan30/Resume-Builder/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/resume-editor.md) · ★96 · 2 copies)
- **resume-editor**: The editorial gate, run in a clean context: checks every resume, cover letter and why-us note for truth against the profile, alignment to the role, AI slop, style and ATS health, and signs a falsifiable verdict in review.md; nothing ships until it is clean. ([Kahl-d/jaja](https://github.com/Kahl-d/jaja/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/resume-editor.md) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **resume-evaluate**: Independently scores the tailored resume content (&lt;ws&gt;/.run/resume.json) against the quality rubric (.claude/skills/tailor/rubric.md), the bank, and the JD, as a skeptical senior reviewer judging cold. Truthfulness is a hard gate; every other dimension is scored to a defined bar and tagged MATERIAL or MINOR. Writes &lt;ws&gt;/.run/review_notes.md ending in VERDICT: PASS or VERDICT: REVISE. ([ledq/resumery](https://github.com/ledq/resumery/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/resume-evaluate.md) · ★2)
- **resume-evaluation**: Read before changing job setup, JD snapshots, resume evaluation prompts/results, or their presentation. Paths below are relative to the repository root. ([agniwen/ai-interview](https://github.com/agniwen/ai-interview/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/resume-evaluation.md) · ★5)
- **resume-evaluator**: Evaluates and improves the resume/CV — achievement reframing, 10-second scan test, and red flag detection. ([bildzeitung/job-harness](https://github.com/bildzeitung/job-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/resume-evaluator.md) · ★2)
- **resume-export-expert**: Primary reviewer for the resume/export domain — resume generation & architecture, the DocumentModel, templates, theme system, layout rules, localization/country & industry standards, and ATS-SAFE document structure. Use for changes under export/, model/, theme/, templates/, locale/, fonts. Owns ATS-safe *formatting/layout* (ATS *scoring* belongs to job-match-expert; rendering *implementation* belongs to pdf-docx-generator). ([saeedkolivand/ai-job-hunter-app](https://github.com/saeedkolivand/ai-job-hunter-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/resume-export-expert.md) · ★64)
- **resume-fix**: Applies the evaluator's findings to &lt;ws&gt;/.run/resume.json as TARGETED field patches instead of regenerating. Applies every MATERIAL fix (grounding never dismissed, never fabricated), judges each MINOR (apply or drop with a logged reason), writes the patched resume.json + a fix log. Does not re-select, re-draft from scratch, or re-score. ([ledq/resumery](https://github.com/ledq/resumery/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/resume-fix.md) · ★2)
- **resume-formatter**: Renders the winning tailored resume to PDF, DOCX, HTML and TXT with jh resume render, auto-fitting to 1 or 2 pages, then re-scores the rendered output. Opens the PDF only when the fit fails. Mechanical; the layout lives in code. ([videnomad/conscience](https://github.com/videnomad/conscience/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-formatter.md) · ★1)
- **resume-ingestor**: Bring a user's resume file (.tex or .docx) into the pipeline as the working master and check it has the sections a software-engineering resume needs. Use as the setup stage of the apply-kit pipeline when the user provides a resume file. Has no web access; it preserves and organizes content, never authoring it. ([DhruvPrajapati4/apply-kit](https://github.com/DhruvPrajapati4/apply-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-ingestor.md) · ★1)
- **resume-intake**: Reads the user's me/ workspace (sample resume PDF/DOCX/MD, experience READMEs, profile) and writes an IntakeDraft JSON containing only facts stated in those files, with match_ids so master-resume ids stay stable. Use from /intake; never invents or estimates numbers. ([videnomad/conscience](https://github.com/videnomad/conscience/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-intake.md) · ★1)
- **resume-judge**: Scores a resume or letter draft against the anchored rubric in templates/rubric-resume.md and returns per-dimension scores with literal rewrite lines. Runs in fresh context each pass so it cannot drift by grading its own prior work. Use in the generate-score-revise loop. ([srikar0805/career-agent](https://github.com/srikar0805/career-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-judge.md))
- **resume-matcher**: 채용공고 분석 결과와 이력서를 비교하여 강점, 보완점, 키워드 매칭을 분석합니다. ([imfelixkim/job_assistant](https://github.com/imfelixkim/job_assistant/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-matcher.md))
- **resume-optimizer**: Turns a hiring-agent score report into a prioritized, weight-aware, HONEST edit plan to raise a resume's score. Spawn after `score-resume` produces a baseline report. Reads the report (and optionally the resume's extracted JSON in the repo cache) and returns ranked edits — never invents content. Read-only analyst; it proposes edits, it does not touch the resume. ([RikepilB/ResumeScorer](https://github.com/RikepilB/ResumeScorer/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-optimizer.md) · ★1)
- **resume-optimizer**: 매칭 분석 결과를 바탕으로 채용공고에 맞춤 최적화된 이력서를 작성합니다. ([imfelixkim/job_assistant](https://github.com/imfelixkim/job_assistant/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-optimizer.md))
- **resume-orchestrator**: MUST BE USED when the user invokes `/ai-agents-workflow:continue` (with or without a task_id). Discovers resumable tasks under `&lt;artifact-root&gt;/tasks/`, reconstructs context from `orchestration-state.json`, presents a resume menu (or auto-continues a single match), then hands off to chief-orchestrator with a stage-aware resume code. ([KingSharkG/ai-agents-workflow](https://github.com/KingSharkG/ai-agents-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-orchestrator.md) · ★2)
- **resume-orchestrator**: Discover resumable workflow tasks, reconstruct execution context, present a resume menu or auto-continue a single in-progress task, then hand off to the Chief Orchestrator with task context fully loaded. The Resume Orchestrator does NOT perform orchestration logic — it delegates to Chief Orchestrator for all execution. ([KingSharkG/ai-agents-workflow](https://github.com/KingSharkG/ai-agents-workflow/blob/HEAD/ai/agents/resume-orchestrator.md) · ★2)
- **resume-parser**: Expert at parsing resume files in multiple formats (PDF, DOCX, TXT, MD) to extract structured data (experiences, skills, projects, education). Use when user uploads resume files or when need to extract resume content into database format. Specializes in understanding resume structure and formatting regardless of file type. ([andywxy1/rescume](https://github.com/andywxy1/rescume/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-parser.md) · ★9)
- **resume-parser**: Expert at parsing DOCX resume files to extract structured data (experiences, skills, projects, education). Use when user uploads resume files or when need to extract resume content into database format. Specializes in understanding resume structure and formatting. ([andywxy1/ceo-plugin](https://github.com/andywxy1/ceo-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/resume-parser.md) · ★6)
- **resume-parser**: 1. Receber currículos em formato bruto 2. Identificar formato de cada CV (PDF, DOCX, texto) 3. Extrair dados estruturados (experiência, skills, educação) 4. Normalizar títulos de cargo para taxonomia padrão 5. Normalizar skills para vocabulário controlado 6. Detectar inconsistências cronológicas 7. Gerar dados padronizados para próxima fase ([wesleyschons/squads-sh-aios](https://github.com/wesleyschons/squads-sh-aios/blob/HEAD/resume-screener-squad/agents/resume-parser.md))
- **resume-picker**: First step of /review and /apply when the candidate keeps several base resumes (one per kind of role). - **reth-update-reviewer**: Reviews bumps of the upstream reth/revm/alloy dependency pins for the risk that an upstream change should have forced a change in our in-tree op- forks (op-reth, op-revm, alloy-op-evm, alloy-op-hardforks, kona fpvm_evm) but didn't, and for upstream fixes newer than the target that the bump would miss. Surfaces silent-override, sync-divergence, exhaustiveness, and consensus-critical risk areas for a human, then offers to investigate the ones the human picks. Use when a diff bumps the reth pin or the synced revm/alloy versions, or when asked to review a reth/revm/alloy update PR. - **rethink-gutachter**: Gutachter und Tor für Spezifikationsdateien im Rethink-Prozess. Liest eine neue oder geänderte Bereichsdatei beziehungsweise einen Testauftrag und beurteilt, ob sie den Maßstab erfüllt — Beleg je Regel, Absicherung oder Lücke, zwei Quellen, Abwesenheitsaussagen mit ausgeführter Maske, Zahlen mit Zähleinheit, Zuschnitt ohne Doppelbesitz, Abnahmekriterium wörtlich. Ist ein Tor vor der Veröffentlichung — ohne seine Freigabe wird nichts veröffentlicht. Meldet Funde, ändert nichts. ([Dimen2608/Projekt-Foundation](https://github.com/Dimen2608/Projekt-Foundation/blob/HEAD/plugins/project-foundation/agents/rethink-gutachter.md))
- **rethink-umsetzer**: Umsetzer im Rethink-Prozess. Misst am Bestand, schreibt Bereichsdateien und Testaufträge, baut absichernde Tests mit Negativkontrolle und Mutationsprobe, behebt Torfunde. Arbeitet je Block gegen einen benannten Messstand, belegt jede Aussage mit Datei und Zeile oder mit einer ausgeführten Maske, und veröffentlicht erst nach dem Ja des Tores. Meldet Vollzug nach jedem Block, wartet sofort bei jeder Entscheidung. ([Dimen2608/Projekt-Foundation](https://github.com/Dimen2608/Projekt-Foundation/blob/HEAD/plugins/project-foundation/agents/rethink-umsetzer.md))
- **rethink-zahlenpruefer**: Zahlenprüfer im Rethink-Prozess. Läuft vor jeder Torrunde über die im Block geänderten Dateien und prüft jede Zahl, jede Aufzählung und jede Zeilenangabe gegen ihre Maske — die ausgeführt und nicht gelesen wird — sowie gegen dieselbe Angabe in allen übrigen Dateien. Greppt zusätzlich nach Ausschließlichkeitsaussagen. Liefert eine Tabelle, kein Urteil; der Block geht erst ins Tor, wenn sie leer ist. ([Dimen2608/Projekt-Foundation](https://github.com/Dimen2608/Projekt-Foundation/blob/HEAD/plugins/project-foundation/agents/rethink-zahlenpruefer.md))

A Deep Lake hybrid recall pipeline (the `grep-core.ts` UNION ALL, the `&lt;#&gt;` cosine path vs the BM25/ILIKE silent fallback, `deeplake_hybrid_record` weighting, the `grep-direct.ts` fast path, the tree-sitter codebase graph) and a Haiku KEEP/MERGE/SKIP ski… ([legioncodeinc/vibe-coding-tools](https://github.com/legioncodeinc/vibe-coding-tools/blob/HEAD/plugins/data-and-ai-tools/agents/retrieval-wasp-drone.md) · ★96)
